Honestly, sometimes I feel like dying would be the fair punishment for what I've done to my family and my crew.\n\nDoctor: Have you thought about how you would carry out your death if you were to act on these feelings?\n\nPatient: I don't really have a specific plan. I just have this constant feeling that I deserve it, that dying is the only fair punishment for everything I've put my family and my crew through.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been having these thoughts about not waking up?\n\nPatient: It's been with me for about a year and two months now. I used to do a lot of things... I'd paint, cook these big meals, go for a run before my flooring jobs, and I'd always show up for theater rehearsals. I mean, I don't feel nervous or anything like that. It's more just... this heavy feeling all the time. My work pants have gotten really loose on me lately, and I haven't been trying to lose weight or anything. Most days I just... I forget to eat until it's really late. I mean, I'm tired all the time, but I wouldn't say I'm physically moving in slow motion or anything like that. I just... It really takes a toll on me... just getting through the day is incredibly hard. I don't know, those things just feel like chores from somebody else's life.
